PHASE: Hamlet meets the CW requirements
PHASE_SET: 2026-09-25
DESCRIPTION: CW_REQUIREMENTS.md and CW_SPEC.md at the repository root are the specification from here on. Sixty-eight requirements, sixty-five of them must-tier, and section T shows fourteen groups with no test at all. This phase traces what exists, builds the metrics the requirements are written in - invented characters, sure-character error, coverage, word-boundary error, acquisition time - and then meets the requirements group by group, highest tier first. Every unit recenters on those two documents. Judged by requirement ids and, at the end, by Tim at the radio.
STEP: 0 | done | Every CW test is traced - section T names, for each requirement, the test that proves it or the word none, and every existing CW test names the requirement it proves or is marked as proving none.
STEP: 1 | done | The metrics exist - MET-INVENTED, MET-CER-SURE, MET-COVERAGE and MET-WBE are computed over the corpus and reported per condition, so the requirements can be measured at all.
STEP: 2 | done | The record is honest - section A, the nine requirements about what the decoder claims and refuses to claim.
STEP: 3 | partial | Confidence means something - section B, the three classes and their rates, including the dim class the decoder does not have today.
STEP: 4 | partial | The pitch is right - section J, and the acquisition requirements of section K that depend on it.
STEP: 5 | partial | The speed is right - section D, five to forty-five words a minute, acquired cold and tracked through a change.
STEP: 6 | partial | The text is right - sections H and I, the character table, the prosigns and the word boundaries.
STEP: 7 | partial | The conditions can be generated - sections E, F and G: channel, sender and interference profiles, which nothing in the tree produces today.
STEP: 8 | not started | Tim at the radio - CW on 20 m or 40 m, text on the CW tab that reads as what was sent, and he says it read.
STEP: 9 | partial | Two decoders read the same audio - section M: fldigi's CW modem ported faithfully as the second decoder, both scored on every recording before either votes, both confidences calibrated against the keys, and the arbitration that emits one transcript, never worse than the better decoder alone.
STEP: 11 | partial | The owner's ear teaches the detector where the entry is - a light saying whether Hamlet thinks it hears CW, a strip showing the whole pitch range and where it looks, two buttons that write his verdict beside the detector's state; no decode, no corpus.
STEP: 12 | not started | The oscilloscope - a keyed signal is found as the audio envelope over a threshold above the tracked noise floor, at any pitch the filter passes, its frequency read from the spectrum after; shown on the CW tab first, then driving the decoder.

## UNIT 492 - STEP 12

Run by hand, outside the loop; SESSION.lock taken and released, nothing written to RUN_LEDGER.md, nothing under tools\arbiter\ touched, no box ticked, no scratch file committed. Version 1.13.178 to 1.13.179. **A mark is handed out when it ends (R105):** CwEnvelopeDetector hands out every completed bar in every bin, paired or not, one envelope window after it ends and no later than three, where it used to wait to be paired and on the wander check's one-second window; pairing and the wander check still drive the keying verdict, the light and the scope, whose counts did not move. Three rules were needed and are stated in the code: a mark ends where its tone ends (one window after it, the peak of its lobe has dropped below it by more than the flatness floor), so a shoulder bin's piece of a dah that stops while the tone goes on is not handed out ahead of the whole dah; a mark begins where its tone rose, reaching back over the peak's hops at its level, because the key-down edge can split a dah's top; and a mark already called stands in for a new one only at the same level. **The noise guard moved to the run reader:** a sender is printed only after two runs of two marks or more with dits and dahs among them, a run of one mark never counts toward one, and a sender that never gets there is forgotten after a second of silence; once printed, a one-mark letter prints with it. Worst delivery went from 85, 445 and 710 ms on the clean call, the call with bursts and the two-station case to 15 ms on each; the call with bursts now reads `CQ CQ DE N0CALL N0CALL K` whole (unit 491: `CQ CQ DE N0CAAEI D N0CALAI K`), the two-station case reads the call whole (unit 491: `C RE CENT DE N0 FALL N0CALL K`), all 65 of the call's marks are called in every case, loud noise alone hands out 1,457 marks and prints nothing, and a lone dit or dah prints nothing. The owner's answer to unit 491's ask - keep the pairing change - is recorded in PARKED.md. Three one-mark letters in a row are now dropped only where they do not lie inside a word, so DETECT, DETERMINED, KILOMETERS, ATTEMPT and BETTER LETTER read whole at 30 WPM, while five lone E and T still print nothing. ## UNIT 558 - STEP 12

Run by hand, outside the loop: SESSION.lock was taken and released, nothing was written to RUN_LEDGER.md, nothing under tools\arbiter\ was touched, and no box was ticked. HEAD was tagged before-candidate-windows and pushed before any engine change. HM-DEC-262. A candidate's provisional window was built: a sequence of three marks or more that shows two lengths and some shape gets its own window, mixed at its pitch, filtered to its dit and read by level as a standing station's is, the four loudest at once, two bins clear of a station's window and of each other, lost after 4.4 seconds without a mark. Measured on, the twelve read 234 against 237 and the w1aw table 2111 against 2112, so it is in the tree switched off. As first built, with windows on noise and on a station's own shadows, it read 147 and 1314. On, the weak fast station read a little more of its call; the synthetic 13 dB POTA call 200 Hz off the pitch reads whole with it on and off, so the bench does not reproduce the live failure; the QSO replies and W1AW cold read as at HEAD. The cold re-read was not tried again, and the light and the scan were not changed.
